THE CITY · Qwen 27B, rules v2, 400 days, game 3
M6J is mayor from day 5 to the end. A duty of one food every five turns fills the store with 1,727 food; the mayor gives themselves 73 of it, names C2B treasurer and hirer, and nobody dies.
24 founders · 400 days · with a town hall · seed 3 · qwen
